Second transcription's note: Step 1 question 3 (Form 2555) and question 4 (nonresident alien) cannot be answered from supplied inputs. They are covered by refusals: sa.excluded_income for Form 2555 and scope.other_income for nonresident status. Step 5 question 3 and Worksheet B line 5 are the line-1 limit test. Worksheet B line 4b of zero or less, and line 2 or line 7 of zero, are the line-2 test.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi pp. 40-43, Steps 1 to 5, and p. 46, Worksheet A, line 2. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 140 partial records; 2,135 comparisons): 1,945 of 2,135 agree; differences 59 candidate-unknown, 51 project-unknown, 51 value, 24 candidate-refuses, 5 project-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 19 value, 1 candidate-unknown. Completed-return witnesses were listed with the witness cap lifted (triage.mjs run directly; scratch /tmp/adj-bklg-dems/eic.full.json, rerun minutes later while concurrent class M runs were rewriting fixtures/2025/classm*.json, so its counts can differ from the record by a few class M records). On classm.asset.1 (a completed return the project ends at an earlier STOP) the candidate is unknown for dependent.kidnapped, which the project and the interview do not ask there; a candidate that needs an answer the source does not need is a model error (README). The value differences are all on completed returns with no earned income for the credit (Worksheet A line 2 or Worksheet B line 4b zero, "STOP You can't take the credit"): classm.f1040.4, classm.f1040.4.deepseek, classm.f8863.2, classm.f8863.2.deepseek, classm.sli.4, classm.ssb.4, stage14.s8812.eiw_stop_with_se_tax, stage14.s8812.eiw_zero_with_se_tax, stage14.eic.wsb_stop_27c, generated 143, 170, 238, 242, 253, 254, 261, 355, 405, 412 and two partial records of classm.f8863.2.deepseek. The id eic.eligible is a project interface (the prompt lists it without a definition and says "These names are not instructions for how to compute them"); the source has no quantity of that name. The project's reading (label "Steps 1 to 5 passed in order") leaves the worksheet STOPs to eic.line6, and eic.credit agrees on every completed return, so no eic amount differs. The candidate folds the worksheet STOP into the test. The difference does reach a printed result through the backlog package's new gate household.eic_disallowed_claim (household.credit_disallowed and eic.eligible): with the Form 8862 answer Yes, classm.ssb.4 and stage14.eic.wsb_stop_27c, whose EIC is $0, are refused at eic.credit and Form 1040 lines 27a, 34 and 37 (evaluated). i1040gi p. 43: "You must file Form 8862 if your EIC for a year after 1996 was reduced or disallowed", that is, to take the credit, and these filers cannot take it. Finding F1 (`.transcription/proposed/bklg/eic.findings.md`): the gate should read the credit figured (eic.line6 above zero), not eic.eligible. Project's eic.eligible stands as labelled; the finding is in the gate that reads it.

### eic.credit — Earned income credit for Form 1040, line 27a (Worksheet A, line 6, or Worksheet B, line 11
Status: **DISAGREE**

On classm.asset.1 (a completed return the project ends at an earlier STOP) the candidate is unknown for dependent.kidnapped, which the project and the interview do not ask there; a candidate that needs an answer the source does not need is a model error (README). No value differs on a completed return; the remaining differences are refusals and unknowns on refused or incomplete records (scope screens and question order). The refusal gate changed by the package (household.eic_disallowed_claim) is the project's; see finding F1 on eic.eligible. Project stands.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 43, Line 27c, and the STOPs on pp. 40-47 that say to check it. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 140 partial records; 2,135 comparisons): 2,038 of 2,135 agree; differences 72 candidate-unknown, 13 candidate-refuses, 11 project-refuses, 1 value; on returns the full interview completes: 1 candidate-unknown. On classm.asset.1 (a completed return the project ends at an earlier STOP) the candidate is unknown for dependent.kidnapped, which the project and the interview do not ask there; a candidate that needs an answer the source does not need is a model error (README). No value differs on a completed return. Project stands.

On classm.asset.1 and stage14.joint.spouse_wages (completed joint returns, the spouse without self-employment) the candidate is unknown for the spouse's person.se_screen, which the interview does not ask there; a candidate that needs an answer the source does not need is a model error (README). executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 140 partial records; 2,135 comparisons): 568 of 2,135 agree; differences 1143 candidate-shows-skipped, 325 project-refuses, 90 project-unknown, 5 candidate-refuses, 4 candidate-unknown; on returns the full interview completes: 254 candidate-shows-skipped, 2 candidate-unknown. Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A, line 4: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same? Yes. Skip line 5; enter the amount from line 2 on line 6." The candidate has no shown_when and shows a nonzero amount on 2 completed returns where the project hides the line (shown_when eic.eligible); a nonzero amount on a line the filer does not reach is a model error (README, skipped-line rule). Where both sides show the line, no value differs on a completed return. On classm.asset.1 and stage14.joint.spouse_wages (completed joint returns, the spouse without self-employment) the candidate is unknown for the spouse's person.se_screen, which the interview does not ask there; a candidate that needs an answer the source does not need is a model error (README). executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 140 partial records; 2,135 comparisons): 1,919 of 2,135 agree; differences 85 project-unknown, 76 candidate-unknown, 25 candidate-shows-skipped, 17 candidate-refuses, 13 project-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 2 candidate-shows-skipped, 2 candidate-unknown. Project stands.

##### Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same? Yes. Skip line 5; enter the amount from line 2 on line 6. No. Go to line 5."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]
    note: "Open point: line 4 is reached only when line 2 is not zero, because the worksheet says STOP if line 2 is zero. Chosen reading: line 4 is a fixed printed question on Worksheet A, so no shown_when is given and the value is always computed. The effect of the STOP on the credit belongs to line 6, which is reviewed separately."
```

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero amount (a line the worksheet leaves at -0- in this tool's scope)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income of $108.28 or more?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [{const: false}, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "The input interface has no clergy or church-employee question. Those situations (ministers, members of religious orders, Christian Science practitioners, church employee income) are screened out by scope.schedule1 and person.se_screen. So whenever a return is produced, the answer is No. This line is a constant false and refuses when scope.schedule1 is true."

  - id: eic.step5_line1
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    note: "The Clergy and Church employees adjustments to line 1 do not apply because those filers are screened out (see eic.clergy)."

  - id: eic.step5_line2
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.h_zero}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "Medicaid waiver payments are outside the tool (scope.other_income and scope.schedule1), so Schedule 1 line 8s is always zero."

  - id: eic.step5_line3
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.step5_line1, take: eic.step5_line2}

  - id: eic.step5_line4
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.h_zero}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay]
    note: "There is no input for the combat-pay election. Nontaxable combat pay is a screening question (s8812.combat_pay), so the tool computes only the case with no combat pay and refuses otherwise."

  - id: eic.earned_income
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.step5_line3, eic.step5_line4]}

  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 2: Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [s1.has_sc, {const: true}]}
    note: "Self-employment in this tool means having a Schedule C (s1.has_sc). Clergy, church employees and statutory employees (sc.statutory) are screened out, and other self-employment income is excluded by person.se_screen."

  - id: eic.h_se_net
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B lines 1a and 2b: Schedule SE, Part I, line 3 (which equals line 2 here), or Schedule C line 31 for filers not required to file Schedule SE; you and your spouse combined"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.se_line2}
    note: "Farm, partnership, optional-method (line 4b) and church-employee (line 5a) amounts are screened out. That makes Worksheet B line 1b zero, line 2a zero, and SE line 3 equal to SE line 2, which is the Schedule C line 31 result. Read that way, person.se_line2 gives both line 1a for people filing Schedule SE and line 2b for people who do not."

  - id: eic.h_se_ded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 1d: the amount from Schedule SE, Part I, line 13 (persons filing Schedule SE)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.se_line13
      where: {op: test, eq: [person.files_se, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_wsb_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B lines 1e and 2c combined (line 3, statutory employee income, is zero in this tool)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.h_se_net, take: eic.h_se_ded}
    note: "Worksheet B line 3 (statutory-employee Schedule C income) is zero because statutory employees are screened out by sc.statutory."

  - id: eic.wsb_4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.h_wsb_se, eic.earned_income]}

  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: eic.wsb_4b, take: {const: 0}}
    note: "If line 4b is zero or less, the instructions say STOP. Flooring the value at zero makes the table give no credit, and eic.eligible and eic.check_27c carry the STOP."

  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.wsb_6
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.earned_income
    note: "Worksheet A lines 1 to 6 and Worksheet B lines 6 to 11 do the same computation. One chain serves both: for Worksheet B filers (Step 5 question 2 Yes) this line carries Worksheet B line 6, so lines 2 to 6 stand for Worksheet B lines 7 to 11."

  - id: eic.h_n
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_qc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of children who meet the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_has_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 1: Do you have at least one child who meets the conditions to be your qualifying child for the purpose of claiming the EIC?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}

  - id: eic.h_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you filing a joint return for 2025?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}

  - {id: eic.h_lim0_o, printed: "(helper)", label: "$19,104", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.other}}
  - {id: eic.h_lim0_j, printed: "(helper)", label: "$26,214 if married filing jointly", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.joint}}
  - {id: eic.h_lim1_o, printed: "(helper)", label: "$50,434", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.other}}
  - {id: eic.h_lim1_j, printed: "(helper)", label: "$57,554 if married filing jointly", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.joint}}
  - {id: eic.h_lim2_o, printed: "(helper)", label: "$57,310", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.other}}
  - {id: eic.h_lim2_j, printed: "(helper)", label: "$64,430 if married filing jointly", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.joint}}
  - {id: eic.h_lim3_o, printed: "(helper)", label: "$61,555", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.other}}
  - {id: eic.h_lim3_j, printed: "(helper)", label: "$68,675 if married filing jointly", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.joint}}

  - id: eic.h_lim0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, no children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_lim0_j}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_lim0_o}
  - id: eic.h_lim1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 1 child who has a valid SSN"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_lim1_j}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_lim1_o}
  - id: eic.h_lim2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 2 children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_lim2_j}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_lim2_o}
  - id: eic.h_lim3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 3 or more children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_lim3_j}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_lim3_o}

  - id: eic.h_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Income limit for your filing status and number of children who have valid SSNs (Step 1 question 1; Step 5 question 3; Worksheet B line 5)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_n, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_lim0}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_n, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_lim1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_n, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_lim2}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_n, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_lim3}

  - {id: eic.h_l1_c0, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, no qualifying children", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}}
  - {id: eic.h_l1_c1, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, one qualifying child", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}}
  - {id: eic.h_l1_c2, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, two qualifying children", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}}
  - {id: eic.h_l1_c3, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, three or more qualifying children", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}}

  - id: eic.h_l1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1 in the column for your number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_n, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_l1_c0}

  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here. If line 2 is zero, STOP. You can't take the credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, {param: eic.table_start}]}
              - {op: test, ge: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, {param: eic.table_start}]}
                - {op: test, ge: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}
          then: eic.h_l1
    note: "The table starts at $1, and its footnotes say no credit at or above the limit. Amounts outside the table's range therefore give zero, which means STOP."

  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}

  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}

  - {id: eic.h_thr0_o, printed: "(helper)", label: "$10,620", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.other}}
  - {id: eic.h_thr0_j, printed: "(helper)", label: "$17,730 if married filing jointly", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}}
  - {id: eic.h_thr1_o, printed: "(helper)", label: "$23,350", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.other}}
  - {id: eic.h_thr1_j, printed: "(helper)", label: "$30,470 if married filing jointly", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}}
  - id: eic.h_thr0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, no qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_thr0_j}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_thr0_o}
  - id: eic.h_thr1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, 1 or more qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}, then: eic.h_thr1_j}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_thr1_o}
  - id: eic.h_thr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold for your filing status and number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_n, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_thr0}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_n, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_thr1}

  - id: eic.h_below
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5: is the amount on line 3 less than the threshold?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_thr]}

  - id: eic.h_skip5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 left blank (line 4 Yes, or line 5 answered Yes): enter the amount from line 2 on line 6"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below, {const: true}]}

  - {id: eic.h_l3_c0, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, no qualifying children", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}}
  - {id: eic.h_l3_c1, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, one qualifying child", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}}
  - {id: eic.h_l3_c2, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, two qualifying children", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}}
  - {id: eic.h_l3_c3, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, three or more qualifying children", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}}
  - id: eic.h_l3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3 in the column for your number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_n, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_l3_c0}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_n, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_l3_c1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_n, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_l3_c2}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_n, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, then: eic.h_l3_c3}

  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "If No, look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_skip5, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, ge: [eic.line3, eic.h_limit]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_skip5, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, ge: [eic.line3, eic.h_limit]}
          then: eic.h_l3
    note: "When line 5 is left blank it is shown as zero, and line 6 then uses line 2 alone. When line 3 is at or above the Step 1 limit, the table and its footnotes give no credit, and Step 1 already makes the credit unavailable."

  - id: eic.h_min56
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Look at the amounts on lines 5 and 2. Then, enter the smaller amount on line 6."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}

  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6 (eic.line6)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_skip5, {const: true}]}, then: eic.line2}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_skip5, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_min56}

  - id: eic.h_agi_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 1: is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than the limit for the number of children who have valid SSNs who lived with you?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.h_no_ssn
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons on the return (you, and your spouse if filing jointly) without an SSN that allows work and is valid for EIC purposes"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where:
        op: test
        any:
          - {op: test, eq: [person.eic_ssn, {const: false}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [person.work_ssn, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_ssn_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 2: Do you, and your spouse if filing a joint return, have a social security number issued on or before the due date of your 2025 return (including extensions) that allows you to work and is valid for EIC purposes?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_no_ssn, {const: 0, type: Count}]}

  - id: eic.h_7a_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040 line 7a (if line 7a is a loss, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line7a, take: {const: 0}}

  - id: eic.h_inv
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 1: Investment income (lines 2a + 2b + 3b + 7a)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line2a, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, eic.h_7a_pos]}

  - id: eic.h_inv_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 2: Is your investment income more than $11,950? (No)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: [eic.h_inv, {param: eic.investment_limit}]}
    note: "If investment income is over the limit, question 3 asks about Form 4797. Form 4797 is outside this tool (sd.other_forms, scope.schedule1), so the answer is No and the result is STOP. Question 4 (Schedule E, personal property rental, Form 8814, passive activity) is answered No because all of those are screened out (scope.schedule1, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16)."

  - id: eic.h_married_sep
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you a married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_s3_stop5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, questions 3 to 5: married filing separately or head of household, same principal residence for the last 6 months, and not legally separated (STOP)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_step3_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 (filers with a qualifying child) passed: go to Step 5"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_stop5, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_age_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons on the return born after December 31, 1960, and before January 2, 2001"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_after_1960, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_before_2001, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_age_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 2: Were you, or your spouse if filing a joint return, at least age 25 but under age 65 at the end of 2025?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_age_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
    refuses: [eic.died_2025]
    note: "The IRS sends filers whose spouse died in 2025, or who are preparing a return for someone who died in 2025, to Pub. 596, which is not supplied. The tool refuses in that case."

  - id: eic.h_step4_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 (filers without a qualifying child) passed: go to Step 5"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}
    note: "Question 6 (can you be claimed as a dependent on someone else's return) reads sa.dependent. It is the only supplied answer with the same 'not required to file' exception."

  - id: eic.h_path_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 or Step 4 passed, whichever applies"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step3_ok, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step4_ok, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_pre5_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 through 4 passed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_agi_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_inv_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_path_ok, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.eligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You can take the earned income credit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pre5_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}
        - {op: test, gt: [eic.line2, {const: 0}]}
    refuses: [scope.other_income, sa.excluded_income]
    note: "Step 1 question 3 (Form 2555) and question 4 (nonresident alien) cannot be answered from supplied inputs. They are covered by refusals: sa.excluded_income for Form 2555 and scope.other_income for nonresident status. Step 5 question 3 and Worksheet B line 5 are the line-1 limit test. Worksheet B line 4b of zero or less, and line 2 or line 7 of zero, are the line-2 test."

  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit (Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 27a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.eligible, {const: true}]}, then: eic.line6}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.eligible, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_zero}

  - id: eic.h_27c_ssn
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 2 answered No: check the box on line 27c"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_agi_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_ok, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_27c_path
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 question 6 Yes, Step 4 question 3 No, or Step 4 question 5 Yes: check the box on line 27c"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_agi_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_inv_ok, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_stop5, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: false}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_27c_ws
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet STOP with line 27c: Worksheet A line 2 zero; Worksheet B line 4b zero or less, line 5 No, or line 7 zero"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pre5_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.eligible, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}
    note: "Filers who use Worksheet A and fail Step 5 question 3 stop without the line 27c instruction. That is why the line-1 limit test applies only to those filers."

  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 27c"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_27c_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_27c_path, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_27c_ws, {const: true}]}
    note: "The flowchart is followed in order, so a STOP that has no line 27c instruction (Step 1 question 1, Step 2, Step 4 questions 1, 2 and 6, Step 5 question 3) comes before any later 27c instruction. The election not to claim the credit has no input, so it is not modeled."

  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses on page 1 of Form 1040 or 1040-SR"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_agi_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_inv_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: true}]}
    note: "Step 3 question 6 'No' sends every filer to the box. The Special rule for separated spouses limits it to filers who are married and not filing jointly, which is the path through questions 3 to 5. This line follows that narrower reading."
```
```
